\mathbb{W}_{0}^{\infty}(\theta)
is a Bogoliubov transformation.
∎

Appendix C
Fluctuation generator of the dressed dynamics

In this section we provide the derivation of the fluctuation generator of the
dressed Nelson dynamics.

In order to disentangle the calculation, we write the excitation map as

(C.1)

X_{u,\alpha}=X_{u}\otimes W^{*}(\sqrt{N}\alpha),

where
X_{u}:\bigotimes_{\rm sym}^{N}L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\to\mathcal{F}_{\perp u}
acts as

\Psi_{N}\mapsto((X_{u}\Psi_{N})^{(k)})_{k=0}^{N}
with

(C.2)

(X_{u}\Psi_{N})^{(k)}=\binom{N}{k}^{1/2}\prod_{i=1}^{k}(q_{u})_{i}\big\langle u^{\otimes(N-k)},\Psi_{N}\big\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3(N-k)})}\in\mathcal{F}^{(k)}_{\perp u}

We first calculate the result of applying only the Weyl operator.

Lemma C.1
.

For
(u,\alpha)\in H^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\oplus\mathfrak{h}_{5/2}
with

\|u\|_{L^{2}}=1
let
(u_{t},\alpha_{t})=\mathfrak{s}^{\mathrm{D}}[t](u,\alpha)
be the solution to (
3.3
).
Let

(C.3)

H_{\alpha}^{\mathrm{D},\leq N}(t)=i\dot{W}(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})+W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}H_{N}^{\rm D}W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})

then

(C.4a)

\displaystyle H_{\alpha}^{\mathrm{D},\leq N}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big(-\Delta_{x_{j}}+A_{\alpha_{t},x_{j}}+F^{2}_{\alpha_{t}}(x_{j})-\mathrm{Re}\big\langle\alpha_{t},f_{u_{t}}+g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}}\big\rangle\Big)

(C.4b)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i<j}V(x_{i}-x_{j})+\textnormal{d}\Gamma_{a}(\omega)-\sqrt{N}\hat{\Phi}(f_{u_{t}}+g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}})

(C.4c)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\int dk\,kB_{x_{j}}(k)\cdot\big(-i\nabla_{j}+F_{\alpha_{t}}(x_{j})\big)a_{k}^{*}+\mathrm{h.c.}

(C.4d)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\big(a(kB_{x_{j}})^{2}+\textnormal{h.c.}+2a^{*}(kB_{x_{j}})a(kB_{x_{j}})\big)

Proof.

We recall the form of
H^{\mathrm{D}}_{N}
given in
Lemma
3.1
. By the shift property of the Weyl
operator (
2.9
), we have

(C.5a)

\displaystyle W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}\textnormal{d}\Gamma_{a}(\omega)W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})

\displaystyle=\textnormal{d}\Gamma_{a}(\omega)+\hat{\Phi}(\sqrt{N}\omega\alpha_{t})+N\big\langle\alpha_{t},\omega\alpha_{t}\big\rangle,

(C.5b)

\displaystyle W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}\hat{A}_{x_{j}}W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})

\displaystyle=\hat{A}_{x_{j}}+\sqrt{N}A_{\alpha_{t},x_{j}}.

with
A_{\alpha_{t},x}=-2i\nabla_{x}\cdot\big\langle kB_{x},\alpha_{t}\big\rangle+\text{h.c.}
, and

\displaystyle W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}\big(a(kB_{x_{j}})^{2}+\textnormal{h.c.}+2a^{*}(kB_{x_{j}})a(kB_{x_{j}})\big)W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})

\displaystyle\ =\big(a(kB_{x_{j}})^{2}+\textnormal{h.c.}+2a^{*}(kB_{x_{j}})a(kB_{x_{j}})\big)

(C.5c)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sqrt{N}F_{\alpha_{t}}(x_{j})\hat{\Phi}(kB_{x_{j}})+NF^{2}_{\alpha_{t}}(x_{j}),

where we inserted
F_{\alpha_{t}}(x)=2\mathrm{Re}\big\langle kB_{x},\alpha_{t}\big\rangle
and used

(C.6)

\displaystyle 2\big|\big\langle\alpha_{t},kB_{x_{j}}\big\rangle\big|^{2}+\big\langle\alpha_{t},kB_{x_{j}}\big\rangle^{2}+\big\langle kB_{x_{j}},\alpha_{t}\big\rangle^{2}=\big(2\mathrm{Re}\big\langle kB_{x_{j}},\alpha_{t}\big\rangle\big)^{2}.

The operators
-\Delta_{x_{j}}
and
V(x_{j}-x_{i})
are left unchanged by

W(\sqrt{N}\alpha)
, so it remains to the term with the time-derivative.
We use the formula
W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}=e^{a(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})}e^{-a^{*}(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})}e^{\frac{N}{2}\|\alpha_{t}\|^{2}}
to compute

\displaystyle\tfrac{d}{dt}W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}

\displaystyle=e^{a(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})}a(\sqrt{N}\dot{\alpha}_{t})e^{-a^{*}(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})}e^{\frac{N}{2}\|\alpha_{t}\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad-e^{a(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})}e^{-a^{*}(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})}e^{\frac{N}{2}\|\alpha_{t}\|^{2}}\big(a^{*}(\sqrt{N}\dot{\alpha}_{t})-N\mathrm{Re}\big\langle\dot{\alpha}_{t},\alpha_{t}\big\rangle\big)

(C.7)

\displaystyle=W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}\Big(a(\sqrt{N}\dot{\alpha}_{t})-a^{*}(\sqrt{N}\dot{\alpha}_{t})-N\mathrm{Im}\big\langle\dot{\alpha}_{t},\alpha_{t}\big\rangle\Big)

where the last step follows from

(C.8)

\displaystyle e^{a^{*}(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})}a(\sqrt{N}\dot{\alpha}_{t})e^{-a^{*}(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})}=a(\sqrt{N}\dot{\alpha}_{t})-N\big\langle\dot{\alpha}_{t},\alpha_{t}\big\rangle.

Inserting the equation of motion (
5.2
)
for
\alpha_{t}
, we find

(C.9)

\displaystyle i\tfrac{d}{dt}W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}=W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}\big(-\sqrt{N}\hat{\Phi}(i\dot{\alpha}_{t})+N\mathrm{Re}\big\langle\alpha_{t},i\dot{\alpha}_{t}\big\rangle\big)

\displaystyle=W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*}\big(-\sqrt{N}\hat{\Phi}(\omega\alpha_{t}+f_{u_{t}}+g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}})+N\mathrm{Re}\big\langle\alpha_{t},\omega\alpha_{t}+f_{u_{t}}+g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}}\big\rangle\big),

and with the shift property of the Weyl operator (
2.9
), we get

(C.10)

(i\tfrac{d}{dt}W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})^{*})W(\sqrt{N}\alpha_{t})\\
=-\sqrt{N}\hat{\Phi}(\omega\alpha_{t}+f_{u_{t}}+g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}})-N\mathrm{Re}\big\langle\alpha_{t},\omega\alpha_{t}+f_{u_{t}}+g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}}\big\rangle.

Noting that the terms involving
\omega\alpha_{t}

in (
C.10
) and (
C.5a
) cancel gives the
claim.
∎

The excitation map for the particles
X_{u}
satisfies a general transformation
property.

Lemma C.2
.

For any densely defined operator
B:D(B)\subseteq L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\to L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
and
u\in D(B)
we
have

\displaystyle X_{u}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}B_{j}\bigg)X_{u}^{*}=\big\langle u,Bu\big\rangle(N-\mathcal{N}_{b})

\displaystyle\quad+\int dx\bigg((q_{u}Bu)(x)b_{x}^{*}\sqrt{[N-\mathcal{N}_{b}]_{+}}+\overline{(q_{u}B^{*}u)}(x)\sqrt{[N-\mathcal{N}_{b}]_{+}}b_{x}\bigg)

(C.11)

\displaystyle\quad+\int dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}Bb_{y}

as an operator identity on
\mathcal{F}^{(\leq N)}_{\perp u}
.

Proof.

This follows by writing
\sum_{j}B_{j}
as the restriction to

L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{\otimes N}
of
\mathrm{d}\Gamma(B)
and using the identities
of Lemma
2.1
.
∎

With this, we can now give the proof of the formula for the fluctuation
Hamiltonian.

Proof of Lemma
6.10
.

We first calculate

(C.12)

H_{u,\alpha}^{\mathrm{D},\leq N}(t)=X_{u_{t}}H_{\alpha}^{\mathrm{D},\leq N}(t)X_{u_{t}}^{*}+i\dot{X}_{u_{t}}X_{u_{t}}^{*}

and then add convenient terms that vanish on
\mathcal{F}_{\perp u_{t}}^{\leq N}\otimes\mathcal{F}
to obtain the symmetric expression for

H_{u,\alpha}^{\mathrm{D}}(t)
.

The terms in
H_{\alpha}^{\mathrm{D},\leq N}(t)
that are not either
invariant under
X_{u}
or of the well-known form arising for many-boson
systems are those of lines (
C.4c
) and
(
C.4d
). Using
\overline{B_{x_{j}}(k)}=B_{x_{j}}(-k)
we
write

(C.13)

\big(a(kB_{x_{j}})^{2}+\textnormal{h.c.}+2a^{*}(kB_{x_{j}})a(kB_{x_{j}})\big)\\
=\int dkdl\,kB_{x_{j}}(k)\cdot lB_{x_{j}}(l)\underbrace{\big[-2a_{k}^{*}a_{-l}+a_{k}^{*}a_{l}^{*}+a_{-k}a_{-l}\big]}_{=:\mathcal{A}_{kl}}

and by Lemma
C.2

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}X_{u_{t}}\Big(\int dkdl\,\sum_{j=1}^{N}\big(kB_{x_{j}}(k)\cdot lB_{x_{j}}(l)\big)\,\mathcal{A}_{kl}\Big)X_{u_{t}}^{*}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{N}\int dkdl\underbrace{\big\langle u_{t},kB_{(\cdot)}(k)\cdot lB_{(\cdot)}(l)u_{t}\big\rangle}_{=M_{u_{t}}(k,l)}(N-\mathcal{N}_{b})\,\mathcal{A}_{kl}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{1}{N}\int dkdldx\bigg(\underbrace{(q_{u_{t}}kB_{(\cdot)}(k)\cdot lB_{(\cdot)}(l)u_{t})(x)}_{=N_{u_{t}}(x,k,l)}b_{x}^{*}\sqrt{[N-\mathcal{N}_{b}]_{+}}+\text{h.c}

(C.14)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{1}{N}\int dkdldxdy\underbrace{(q_{u_{t}}kB_{(\cdot)}(k)\cdot lB_{(\cdot)}(l)q_{u_{t}})(x,y)}_{=Q_{u_{t}}(x,y,k,l)}b_{x}^{*}b_{y}\,\mathcal{A}_{kl}.

Recalling the formulas (
3.4e
), (
3.4f
), and
(
4.2a
) for
f_{u_{t}}(k)
,
g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}}(k)
, and

L_{\alpha}(k)
, we also find

(C.15)

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}X_{u_{t}}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\int dk\,kB_{x_{j}}(k)\cdot\big(-i\nabla_{j}+F_{\alpha_{t}}(x_{j})\big)a_{k}^{*}+\text{h.c.}\bigg)X_{u_{t}}^{*}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\int dk\,X_{u_{t}}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{N}kB_{x_{j}}(k)\cdot\big(-i\nabla_{j}+F_{\alpha_{t}}(x_{j})\big)\bigg)X_{u_{t}}^{*}a_{k}^{*}+\text{h.c.}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\int dk\big(f_{u_{t}}(k)+g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}}(k)\big)(N-\mathcal{N}_{b})\,a_{k}^{*}+\text{h.c.}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\int dkdx(q_{u_{t}}L_{\alpha_{t}}(k)u_{t})(x)b_{x}^{*}a_{k}^{*}\sqrt{[N-\mathcal{N}_{b}]_{+}}+\text{h.c.}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\int dkdx(\overline{q_{u_{t}}L_{\alpha_{t}}(k)^{*}u_{t})}(x)\sqrt{[N-\mathcal{N}_{b}]_{+}}b_{x}a_{k}^{*}+\text{h.c.}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}\int dkdxdy\underbrace{(q_{u_{t}}kB_{(\cdot)}(k)\cdot(-i\nabla+F_{\alpha_{t}})q_{u_{t}})(x,y)}_{J_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}}(x,k,y)}b_{x}^{*}b_{y}\,a_{k}^{*}+\text{h.c.}

The term
\sqrt{N}\hat{\Phi}(f_{u_{t}}+g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}})
from the first line
cancels with the corresponding term in
H_{\alpha}^{\mathrm{D},\leq N}(t)
,
and the remaining term
-N^{-1/2}\mathcal{N}_{b}\hat{\Phi}(f_{u_{t}}+g_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}})
equals
H_{2}(t)
from the formula for

H^{\rm D}_{u,\alpha}(t)
. We thus have

(C.16)

H_{u,\alpha}^{\mathrm{D},\leq N}(t)=i\dot{X}_{u_{t}}X_{u_{t}}^{*}\\
+X_{u_{t}}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{N}h_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i<j}V(x_{i}-x_{j})\Big)X_{u_{t}}^{*}+\sum_{j=2}^{5}H_{j}(t).

From
[
66
, Eq.(40)]
and the equation satisfied by
u_{t}
, we
deduce

(C.17)

i\dot{X}_{u_{t}}X_{u_{t}}^{*}=b^{*}(u_{t})b(q_{u_{t}}h_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}}u_{t})-\langle ih_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}},u_{t}\rangle(N-\mathcal{N}_{b})\\
-\sqrt{N-\mathcal{N}_{b}}b(q_{u_{t}}h_{u_{t},\alpha_{t}}u_{t})-\text{h.c}.

This combines with the second term above to yield
H_{0}(t)+H_{1}(t)
as in the
analogous computations in
[
12
,
66
]
. Taking into
account the obvious modifications from replacing
v\mapsto\tfrac{N-1}{N}V
, one can use for instance
[
12
, Eq. (2.20)]
. .
∎
